Red cases inherited from the plan of unit B: a channel without its provider's fields is rejected with a named error (`CREDENTIALS_INCOMPLETE:<provider>:<field>`), not with a generic `AUTH`; the resolver reads no app keys from the blob (a blob with `appSecret` is ignored, measured with a spy on the service); the worker and the API resolve the same row into the same object; the class of the Telegram credentials follows the open point of ADR-0034. Closes DEF-36 and DEF-38; its own chain of at least 3 PRs. ADR-0034 (decisions 1 and 2). 🔗 dep:DEF-37, CRED-8a.

The mechanics of SUP-1 (template, generated index and non-features list) are on `main` since 2026-10-07 (#474 to #476), before the retro pass that writes the pages.**
> **The register and its decisions** — the request of 2026-10-06, the page with its mandatory sections, its stamp and its `paths:`, one home per kind of truth, the answers Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4 and Q5 (2026-10-06), the method and ledger shared with §5.10 and the retirement of `EXCEPTIONS.md` (2026-10-07) — are [ADR-0043](../technical/ADR-0043-support-documentation-register.md); the shared method is [ADR-0042](../technical/ADR-0042-legal-facts-register-method.md).
>

The table of dated exceptions `EXCEPTIONS.md` was retired on 2026-10-07 (the Product Owner's decision, ADR-0043 decision 8): it is absorbed by `NON_FEATURES.md`, where every candidate of the tree is `documented`, `non-feature` with its reason or `pending`, and by the grammar of `## Support impact`, which declares `none for <feature> — <reason>` in each PR. **DoD:** template, index and `NON_FEATURES.md` on `main`; the first two pages (SUP-4, slice 1) written with it; the regenerated index matches the committed one. **Progress 2026-10-07 (#474 to #476):** the front-matter reader, `scripts/support/lib/front-matter.mjs`, with new options in the LEGAL-4 inventory library (#474); the template `docs/support/_TEMPLATE.md`, the generated index `docs/support/README.md` and `pnpm support:index` / `pnpm check:support` (#475); `docs/support/NON_FEATURES.md` with its classification `docs/support/classification/non-features.json` —190 candidates (82 routes, 51 packages, 3 workers, 19 queues, 16 Admin sections and 19 client sections): 6 non-features, 184 pending and 0 pages—, the battery's `support` step and the `docs/support/` row in `CLAUDE.md` §Documentation Policy (#476). **CI verification (fitness #46, #481):** on `main` since 2026-10-07 (`76371939`): `pnpm check:support` in the `dependency-consistency` job of `fitness.yml`, the twin of #45 (`::error` + `exit 1`), with its `# 46.` block in `CLAUDE.md` (46 checks, #1-#46) and the `support` row of the gates table of `docs/development/TESTING_REFOUNDATION.md`; the pending reservations of that table move to #47 (verdict, WU-3.9/3.10), #48 (script entrypoints, WU-3.13) and #49 (environment identity, WU-4b.7). Still missing: the first two pages (SUP-4, slice 1). `EXCEPTIONS.md` is no longer missing because it was retired (above); its stale entry in the list of companion pages of `scripts/support/index.mjs` (`COMPANIONS`) left with #483. The review left SB-098 to SB-103 in `docs/reports/review-suggestions-book.md`: SB-098 to SB-102 implemented by #483 (a single table renderer with the `None.` sentinel for an empty section, strict rejection tables in the three support suites, and the scope error cases with classification problems and with a route planted outside the convention); SB-103 (whether the six inventory suites keep comparing the committed page) waits for a decision; SB-104 (the dated history of the Architecture row of the gates table, to ADR-0032) is a pending docs unit.

> - [ ] **SUP-3** `[M]` **CI** — the gate `scripts/testing/support-docs-gate.mjs`, with its suite in `apps/api/tests/unit/scripts/`, in four parts: (a) coverage —every feature of the derived inventory has a page with all its sections, each member is claimed once and a name that no longer exists fails—; (b) freshness —it fails with 5 or more commits on its `paths` after the stamp or at 90 days, and warns from the first commit or at 60 days; in a PR, a change under a declared `paths` requires the page changed with its stamp inside the PR or the declaration `none for … — reason`—; (c) links —every cited code path exists and, if it carries `#symbol`, the symbol is there—; (d) index —the committed table matches the derivation—. The thresholds live in two constants of the script and are reviewed after three months with the trigger counts (Q5). ### 5.16 Foundation practices for the next application — continuous since 2026-10-15

> **ℹ️ CARD [FOUNDATION] — decided on 2026-10-15; continuous; the Product Owner approved starting it before N-TEST-1 closes because its purpose is to capture the lessons of the Testing Refoundation while they happen.**
> **The document and its rule:** what the document is — the practices the next application adopts from its first commit, on the same stack, domain-agnostic, practices and never packages, strict first, four fields per entry, and later a prompt, a skeleton and the template, accepted only when each gate shows a real red on a planted violation — lives in [FOUNDATION_PRACTICES.md](../development/FOUNDATION_PRACTICES.md) (requested by the Maintainer on 2026-10-15); the rule that every ODD unit that adds an ADR, adds or changes a gate or fitness check, or demolishes and redoes code records its practice in the same pull request, or declares `none for foundation practices — <reason>`, is the "Foundation practices flow" paragraph of [CLAUDE.md](../../CLAUDE.md) §Documentation Policy.
>
> **Out of scope, on purpose:** the harvest of phases 0 and 1 of N-TEST-1, available in `docs/development/TESTING_REFOUNDATION.md`, which can be brought forward as a unit of its own if the Product Owner asks for it; the harvest of the fitness checks, the ADRs and the operational gotchas, which is done when the document is transformed; a section in the PR template, and for now a gate for the flow.
>
> - [~] **FND-1** `[S]` **DOCS** — the document with its eight seed entries, the flow paragraph in `CLAUDE.md`, the row of the `docs/README.md` index and this section. **DoD:** on `main`.
> - [ ] **FND-2** `[continuous]` **DOCS** — each unit that adds an ADR, adds or changes a gate, or demolishes and redoes code records its entry (or declares `none for foundation practices — <reason>` in the PR); the N-TEST-1 units first.
> - [ ] **FND-3** `[M]` **DOCS** — the transformation, when the next application starts: harvest of the fitness checks, the ADRs, the gotchas and phases 0–1 of N-TEST-1; classification of each omni-post gate (by construction, generalized gate or omni-post only); the prompt; the generated skeleton, accepted when each gate shows a real red on a planted violation.

### 5.17 Artifact language and role-based attribution — one dedicated run, decided 2026-10-28

> **ℹ️ CARD [ARTIFACT-LANGUAGE] — decided on 2026-10-28 (D1 to D7); running since 2026-10-28 as one dedicated run, outside the route's queue (D1).**
> Requested by the Product Owner on 2026-10-28, with three goals: English is the only official language of every tracked artifact (code, comments, config, CI, docs and plans); artifacts name roles, never a person, and a person's identity stays only where it is required; and plans and trackers close by moving their living content to permanent homes and then leave the tree with a permalink, so only what survives is translated. A gate holds the debt: fitness #53 counts Spanish lines and personal-name mentions per file against a baseline equal in both directions, and each later unit lowers it in the same diff.
>
> **Decisions (Product Owner, 2026-10-28):**
>
> - **D1 — route position.** The whole workstream runs as one dedicated run, started on the Product Owner's signal; it does not wait in the queue and is not split into a core and later sweeps. Delivery is still chained pull requests, because the native review budget applies. Order: ALR-U0, U1, U2, U3, U4, U5, U6, U8. ALR-U7 joins only once the Testing Refoundation has closed, because its tracker is live while it runs. The other session stays idle during the run, because ALR-U6 rewrites this plan.
> - **D2 — roles.** Two roles. The **Product Owner** decides what the product does: priorities, the route, product and legal decisions. The **Maintainer** decides how it is built and delivered: architecture, merges, releases, and the hooks' grant authority. The canon documents' `**Owner:** Platform engineering` value becomes Maintainers. "Stakeholders" is not a decision-maker: it maps to MADR's `consulted` and `informed`. The data controller of the legal register is the legal entity, not a repository role. `docs/GOVERNANCE.md` maps each role to its holder.
> - **D3 — accepted ADRs.** An editorial role substitution is allowed in accepted ADRs, in the metadata and in the prose; the decision, its status and its rationale are untouched, and the edited ADRs carry no per-file note. `docs/GOVERNANCE.md` records who held each role and since when, so a role named in an old ADR always resolves to a person at that date.
> - **D4 — `openspec/`.** Retired entirely. The archived changes are deleted, with permalinks where they are cited; each active change, the design source of an open item of this plan, moves to `docs/features/<name>.md`; each spec is read once, and a requirement that is still true and pinned nowhere else moves to its feature or canon page.
> - **D5 — dated snapshots.** A dated snapshot leaves the tree, with a permalink wherever it is cited, once no living document stands on it; a snapshot a living document uses as evidence stays, translated.
> - **D6 — notification text.** A separate defect, DEF-151 in §5.11, outside this workstream; until its fix the gate allowlists `InboxEventHandlers.ts` and its strings are not translated.
> - **D7 — anchor checking.** Before any heading is translated, `.lychee.toml` turns on `include_fragments = "anchor-only"`, with its red proven in CI.
> - **Requests in feature documents (option (b)).** An ODD feature document carries only an English translation of each request; the original is kept verbatim in Engram under `odd/<feature>/requests`. The translation becomes the contract when the Product Owner approves the pull request.
> - **B — the detected-smells backlog (2026-10-28).** It folds into this plan and leaves the tree (ALR-U6-9): every open finding becomes a row that carries its SMELL or FN id (DEF-158 to DEF-172 in §5.11, forty rows in §5.1 and three in §5.3, plus the ids added to existing rows), and the §7 closure ledger lists every closed and not-a-defect id.
> - **F1 — the ids of the unmerged N-COR-8 branches (2026-10-29).** They become rows of this plan when each branch lands, and the ids those branches reuse take new ids then; the rule is in the §7 closure ledger, and the [COR] card of §1 points at it.
> - **F2 — findings outside a task's scope (2026-10-29).** The rule moves to CLAUDE.md §Documentation Policy: such a finding becomes a row of this plan in the same pull request, and no SMELL-N row is appended anywhere.
>
> - [x] **ALR-U0** `[S]` **DOCS** — this section and DEF-151; merged by #769 (merge `c8c62cbf`).
> - [ ] **ALR-U1** `[M]` **DOCS** — the convention: ADR-0040, `docs/GOVERNANCE.md`, the language, roles, option (b) and plan-closure rules in CLAUDE.md §Documentation Policy, the MADR fields in the ADR template, and the canon documents' `**Owner:**` value.
> - [ ] **ALR-U2** `[M]` **CI** — the gate, fitness #53: Spanish lines and name mentions per file against an equal-in-both-directions baseline, an allowlist with a reason per entry, and the battery step `artifact-language`.
> - [ ] **ALR-U3** `[M]` **DOCS+CODE** — the production-facing sweep: anchor checking first (D7), then the Prometheus alert texts, the runbooks and the code files that print Spanish.
> - [ ] **ALR-U4** `[S]` **DOCS+HOOKS** — names used as governance terms: CLAUDE.md, the security canon, the legal register, the hook strings and identifiers, and the `Deciders` of the accepted ADRs (D3).
> - [ ] **ALR-U5** `[M]` **DOCS** — the living documents: translate, and replace names with roles.
> - [ ] **ALR-U6** `[L]` **DOCS** — this plan and the product plans: prune closed items, move embedded decisions to ADRs, then translate what remains and rename `MASTER_PLAN_ES.md` to `MASTER_PLAN.md`, repointing every inbound reference; the normalization roadmap folds into §5.2, and the detected-smells backlog into §5.1, §5.3, §5.11 and §7 (B).
> - [ ] **ALR-U7** `[M]` **DOCS** — the Testing Refoundation tracker's closure under the plan-closure clause; at the tracker's closure, outside this run.
> - [ ] **ALR-U8** `[M]` **DOCS** — history: `openspec/` (D4), and the dated reports and audits (D5).

## §7 — Traceability (what this document merged)

This plan replaced `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN_ES.md` (retired 2026-06-29, archived 2026-10-06, deleted from the tree on 2026-10-17; [its last version](https://github.com/edwardricardo/omni-post/blob/2ec1562180ebf61835140dc4f81e70db3d646b89/docs/archive/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN_ES.md)) and `PENDING_WORK_INVENTORY.md` (frozen 2026-07-21, deleted on 2026-10-17) as the single planning source; what it merged, from which document, lives at [this file at `c8c62cbf`](https://github.com/edwardricardo/omni-post/blob/c8c62cbf06c1a38031a0c5a22b7339f120f50497/docs/product/MASTER_PLAN_ES.md), §7. The absorption check of `LATERAL_FINDINGS.md` that §7 left pending closed with P-GATE-1, and the gated documents were deleted with #148 (merge `87decf1a`). On 2026-10-28 it absorbed the normalization roadmap: its open items are the rows of §5.2, four of its follow-ups belong to N-TEST-1, and the rest is history at [its last version](https://github.com/edwardricardo/omni-post/blob/3758be3ee3d4274bcc01582b437080732f50d11d/docs/architecture/NORMALIZATION_ROADMAP.md).

- **Detected-smells backlog — folded 2026-10-29 (ALR-U6-9, decision B of §5.17).** The backlog folded into this plan and left the tree on 2026-10-29; its last version, with every row's full text, is [`roadmap-detected-smells-backlog.md` at `e3fa2af2`](https://github.com/edwardricardo/omni-post/blob/e3fa2af2164705cdbd0124b97007d691a7710223/docs/reports/roadmap-detected-smells-backlog.md) (its last change is `a32347d0`). Each id resolves in this plan: an open finding is the row that carries its id, a slash or range list included (`SMELL-2/5/7/8/33`, `SMELL-60-66`).
